00211: def close_color(a,b):
00212:     return max(abs(a[i]-b[i]) for i in range(3)) < .002
00213: 
00214: for mat in bpy.data.materials:
00215:     if not mat.use_nodes:
00216:         continue
00217:     nodes=mat.node_tree.nodes
00218:     links=mat.node_tree.links
00219:     bs=nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00220:     if bs is None:
00221:         continue
00222:     rgb=tuple(bs.inputs["Base Color"].default_value[:3])
00223:     if close_color(rgb,C["chrome"]):
00224:         bs.inputs["Metallic"].default_value=.72
00225:         bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value=.25
00226:     elif close_color(rgb,C["blue"]):
00227:         bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value=.21
00228:     elif close_color(rgb,C["plastic"]):
00229:         bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value=.32
00230:         bs.inputs["Subsurface Weight"].default_value=.08
00231:     elif close_color(rgb,(.67,.80,.81)):
00232:         bs.inputs["Emission Color"].default_value=(.48,.66,.72,1)
00233:         bs.inputs["Emission Strength"].default_value=.7
00234:     if close_color(rgb,C["granite"]):
00235:         tex=nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00236:         tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value=150
00237:         tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value=2.5
00238:         ramp=nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00239:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position=.25
00240:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color=(.075,.075,.055,1)
00241:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position=.77
00242:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color=(.61,.56,.43,1)
00243:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],ramp.inputs[0])
00244:         links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"],bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00245:         bump=nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00246:         bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value=.23
00247:         bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value=.012
00248:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00249:         links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],bs.inputs["Normal"])
00250:         bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value=.39
00251:     elif close_color(rgb,C["cloth"]) or close_color(rgb,C["paper"]):
00252:         tex=nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00253:         tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value=230
00254:         bump=nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00255:         bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value=.15
00256:         bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value=.006
00257:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])

00156: # Material customization stays procedural; no reference image is used as a texture.
00157: def noise_material(name, base, scale, roughness=.6, strength=.12):
00158:     mat=bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00159:     mat.use_nodes=True
00160:     n=mat.node_tree.nodes
00161:     l=mat.node_tree.links
00162:     bs=n.get("Principled BSDF")
00163:     bs.inputs["Base Color"].default_value=(*base,1)
00164:     bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value=roughness
00165:     tex=n.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00166:     tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value=scale
00167:     tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value=2.5
00168:     bump=n.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00169:     bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value=strength
00170:     bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value=.012
00171:     l.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00172:     l.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],bs.inputs["Normal"])
00173:     return mat
00174:

00213: # Match materials by their finish, since realized primitives have generic names.
00214: def close_color(a,b):
